id="text-47"><div class="widgettitle">Was ist weclapp?</div><div class="textwidget"><p>weclapp ist eine Cloud-basierte ERP-Software, die zu den beliebtesten Lösungen am Markt zählt und als ERP-System des Jahres 2018 ausgezeichnet wurde. Die Software stellt leistungsstarke CRM- und ERP-Funktionalitäten bereit und begeistert mit einfacher Bedienung und intuitiver Nutzerführung. Statt parallel eine CRM-Software, ein Warenwirtschaftssystem und eine Buchhaltungssoftware von unterschiedlichen Anbietern einzusetzen, lassen sich im vollintegrierten ERP-System von weclapp alle Informationen zu Kunden, Projekten, Angeboten, Rechnungen, Artikeln, Bestellungen, etc. spielend einfach innerhalb einer Software verwalten.</p></div></section><section class="widget widget_search" // jquery ui-bit to disable text-select on double-click (used in USP-Slider)
jQuery.support.selectstart = "onselectstart" in document.createElement("div");
jQuery.fn.disableSelection = function() {
  return this.bind( ( jQuery.support.selectstart ? "selectstart" : "mousedown" ) +
    ".ui-disableSelection", function( event ) {
    event.preventDefault();
  });
};

// check if an element is visible in viewport
jQuery.fn.isOnScreen = function(){
  let viewport = {};
  viewport.top = jQuery(window).scrollTop();
  viewport.bottom = viewport.top + jQuery(window).height();
  let bounds = {};
  bounds.top = this.offset().top;
  bounds.bottom = bounds.top + this.outerHeight();
  return ((bounds.top &lt;= viewport.bottom) &amp;&amp; (bounds.bottom &gt;= viewport.top));
};

function setCookie(key, value) {
  let expires = new Date();
  expires.setTime(expires.getTime() + 86400000);
  document.cookie = key + '=' + value + ';expires=' + expires.toUTCString();
}

function getCookie(key) {
  let keyValue = document.cookie.match('(^|;) ?' + key + '=([^;]*)(;|$)');
  return keyValue ? keyValue[2] : null;
}

/* index builder common */
function commonBuildIndex(){
  let indexHTML = '&lt;ul class="common-index"&gt;';
  let anchorID = 1;

  jQuery(".anchor-index-source h2, .anchor-index-source h3, .anchor-index-source h4").each( function(){
    jQuery(this).prepend('&lt;span id="anchorid'+anchorID+'" class="builtAnchor"&gt;&lt;/span&gt;');
    indexHTML = indexHTML + '&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href="#anchorid'+anchorID+'"&gt;'+jQuery(this).text()+'&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;';
    anchorID = anchorID + 1;
  });

  indexHTML = indexHTML + '&lt;/ul&gt;';

  jQuery(".common-index").empty().html(indexHTML);

}

    let icountOne = 0;

    let icountThree = 0;

    let index = 0;

    jQuery(".wiki-texts h1, .wiki-texts h2, .wiki-texts h3, .wiki-texts h4, .wiki-texts h5").each(function() {

  jQuery( ".usp-arrow-fwd" ).on( "click", function( event ) {

    // get rel class of next slide
    let getnext = jQuery(this).parent(".usp-desc").attr("class");
    getnext = getnext.substring(getnext.indexOf("rel") + 3);
    getnext = parseInt(getnext);
    getnext = getnext + 1;
    //console.log(getnext);

    // open next slide
    jQuery(".usp-desc.rel"+getnext).fadeIn().siblings().fadeOut();
    //set new active icon
    jQuery(".usp-icon.rel"+getnext).addClass("active").siblings().removeClass("active");

    event.preventDefault();
    return false;
  });

  // BCK-Button
  jQuery( ".usp-arrow-back" ).on( "click", function( event ) {

    // get rel class of prev slide
    let getnext = jQuery(this).parent(".usp-desc").attr("class");
    getnext = getnext.substring(getnext.indexOf("rel") + 3);
    getnext = parseInt(getnext);
    getnext = getnext -1;
    //console.log(getnext);

    // open next slide
    jQuery(".usp-desc.rel"+getnext).fadeIn().siblings().fadeOut();
    //set new active icon
    jQuery(".usp-icon.rel"+getnext).addClass("active").siblings().removeClass("active");

    event.preventDefault();
    return false;
  });

    }, function() {
      jQuery(this).find(".link-clone").stop(true, true).fadeOut(750, function() {
        jQuery(this).remove();
      });
    }
  );

      jQuery('.carousel-indicators-wrapper').fadeOut();
      jQuery('.carousel-control.left').fadeOut();

const elem = jQuery('.stickybottom');
if(elem.length &gt; 0){
  const tmphtml = elem.html();
  elem.html('&lt;div class="stickybottom-container"&gt;'+tmphtml+'&lt;/div&gt;')
}
jQuery(document).scroll(function(){
  if(elem.length &gt; 0){
    if (
      (jQuery(this).scrollTop() &gt;= 150)
      &amp;&amp;
      (jQuery(this).scrollTop() &lt;= jQuery(document).height()*0.7)
    )
      elem.fadeIn();
    else
      elem.fadeOut();
  };
});


// link the pricing-boxes

if(jQuery('.weclapp-prices-start').length &gt; 0){
  jQuery(".w-pricebox-crm &gt; .ult_pricing_table, .w-pricebox-erpservice &gt; .ult_pricing_table, .w-pricebox-erptrade &gt; .ult_pricing_table").off("click").on("click", function() {

    let wLinkUrl = "https://www.weclapp.com/de/"

    if ( jQuery(this).parent().hasClass("w-pricebox-crm") )
      wLinkUrl = "https://www.weclapp.com/de/cloud-crm/";
    else if ( jQuery(this).parent().hasClass("w-pricebox-erpservice") )
      wLinkUrl = "https://www.weclapp.com/de/cloud-erp-dienstleistung/";
    else if ( jQuery(this).parent().hasClass("w-pricebox-erptrade") )
      wLinkUrl = "https://www.weclapp.com/de/cloud-erp-handel/";

    window.location = wLinkUrl;

  });
}

jQuery(window).load(function() {
  let ehHeight = 0;
  jQuery(".equalheight-container-middle").each(function(){
    ehHeight = jQuery(this).height();
    jQuery(this).find(".vc_column_container").each(function(){
      jQuery(this).children(".wpb_wrapper").css("padding-top", (ehHeight - jQuery(this).children(".wpb_wrapper").height()) / 2 );
    });
  });

  jQuery(".equalheight-container-bottom").each(function(){
    ehHeight = jQuery(this).find(".vc_column_container").height();
    jQuery(this).find(".vc_column_container").each(function(){
      jQuery(this).height(ehHeight).children(".wpb_wrapper").css("padding-top", ehHeight - jQuery(this).children(".wpb_wrapper").height() );
    });
  });

});

jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
  let ehHeight = 0;
  const selector = jQuery(".equalheight-container-middle");
  selector.find(".vc_column_container").children(".wpb_wrapper").css("padding-top", "0");
  selector.each(function(){
    ehHeight = jQuery(this).height();
    jQuery(this).find(".vc_column_container").each(function(){
      jQuery(this).children(".wpb_wrapper").css("padding-top", (ehHeight - jQuery(this).children(".wpb_wrapper").height()) / 2 );
    });
  });

  jQuery(".equalheight-container-bottom").each(function(){
    ehHeight = jQuery(this).find(".vc_column_container").height();
    jQuery(this).find(".vc_column_container").each(function(){
      jQuery(this).height(ehHeight).children(".wpb_wrapper").css("padding-top", ehHeight - jQuery(this).children(".wpb_wrapper").height() );
    });
  });
});

function advBoxSort(arrayI, type) {
  /*
  *
  * type:
  * Alle = -1
  * Unternehmensberatung = 1
  * IT-Diesntleister = 2
  * Einzelhandel = 3
  * Onlineshops = 4
  * Großhandel = 5
  * Produzenten = 6
  * Sonstiges = 7
  *
  */
  let advBoxesFiltered = [];
  let countFiltered = 0;
  if (type !== -1) {
    for (index = 0; index &lt; arrayI.length; ++index) {
      if (arrayI[index].hasClass("branch-"+type)) {
        advBoxesFiltered[countFiltered] = arrayI[index];
        countFiltered = countFiltered + 1;
      }
    }
  } else if (type === -1) {
    advBoxesFiltered = arrayI;
  }

  let ii = 0;
  jQuery(".vc_col-sm-4").each(function(){
    if(advBoxesFiltered[ii] !== undefined) {
      jQuery(this).html(advBoxesFiltered[ii]);
      if (ii % 3 === 0)
        jQuery(this).parents(".vc_row.wpb_row.vc_row-fluid").show();
    } else {
      jQuery(this).empty();
      if (ii % 3 === 0)
        jQuery(this).parents(".vc_row.wpb_row.vc_row-fluid").hide();
    }
    ii = ii + 1;
  });

}

function isScrolledIntoView(elem)
{
  let docViewTop = jQuery(window).scrollTop();
  let docViewBottom = docViewTop + jQuery(window).height();

  const selector = jQuery(elem);
  let elemTop = selector.offset().top;
  let elemBottom = elemTop + selector.height();

  return ((elemBottom &lt;= docViewBottom) &amp;&amp; (elemTop &gt;= docViewTop));
